- **grant-deadline-scout**: Turn a scattered list of grant opportunities into a single prioritized deadline calendar — flagging what's urgent, what's already closed, and where deadlines cluster badly enough to overload writer capacity. Triggers on "grant deadlines", "funding deadlines", "grant submissions", "what grants are due", "grant application status", "research funding deadlines".
